*We get our own place. The old house had too many bad memories. We give our new home a life of its own. We heal. We grow.*
*I wait a long time before I propose. About five years after we first met, I get down on one knee and ask her to spend the rest of her life with me. She freezes for a moment. I start to worry. Then the tears start flowing, and she puts on that big goofy grin.*
*"Uh, obviously. I wasn’t planning on spending it with anyone else."*
*Her parents are thrilled. To my surprise, they love me. Now I get to call someone Mom without feeling sick to my stomach.*
*Mom helps me pick out a suit. Dad helps me pick a venue. It all goes smoothly.*
*We get married in the spring. She can’t decide if she wants to wear a dress or a suit, and ends up wearing both. A suit jacket over a gown. I love it. She writes her own vows, and everybody laughs. In the front row, there are two empty seats. It was her idea.*
*"You never know. They might still show up. It'd be mad awkward if I didn't have a place for them."*
*They don't show up. But somehow, I get the feeling that they know. Wherever they are, I bet they're happy for us. I hope the two of them are as happy as Tara and I are.*
*I kiss Tara on the altar. It feels like I'm kissing her for the first time. It's the most wonderful moment of my life. And as I kiss her, I silently thank Maddie and Abigail. Geladura, too. This wouldn't have been possible without them.*
*That night, I carry Tara across the threshold. She wants it to be as cliché as possible. I have to admit, I like it. It's sweet. It's happy. It's healing.*
*We're complete. And we will be for the rest of our lives.*
*Thank you, Maddie.*
*Thank you, Abigail.*
*Stepping foot in our apartment again elicits a strange mix of emotions. There's the relief of being home, and the elation of having Abigail here with me. But there's a lingering sorrow from one very notable absence.*
*Morgan should be here too.*
*Tara makes jokes, blows off personal inquiries, and avoids showing any sign of the intense pain she's feeling. I have to imagine she's furious with me, too. Everything I say to her is met with hesitation before she responds. She hasn't looked me in the eye for days. She's told me she doesn't blame me, but how could she not? We all know that I could have changed this. When the time came to act, I froze. And Morgan paid for it.*
*There's the thump, thump of scurrying paws from one of the bedrooms as BT hears the door, and both Tara and I smile as we watch the cat bolt down the hall toward us. I can't help but think of Geladura, and I'm sure Tara can't either. Still, Tara kneels down and BT leaps into her arms, nuzzling at her face as she tries to kiss his head.*
*"I missed you too, buddy. It's good to see you again."*
*She places him back on the ground and he makes his way over to me, purring and winding between my legs. I let him show his affection for a minute before he stops and looks up at Abigail. He's normally not great around new people, and I told her to brace for the cold shoulder. Hopefully she's not too disappointed after spending so many years making friends with the forest animals.*
*"Oh, hello! You must be BT! Aren't you the cutest little thing?"*
*Abigail kneels down on the ground. Rather than hiss, he licks her face instead. It seems her affinity for animals wasn't left behind in the forest. Meanwhile, Tara drops her bags and passes us by. "Hey, you guys have fun with the boy. I'm gonna head up to my room."*
*I want to say something. I need to say something. But words fail me. There's nothing I can say or do that I haven't already. I watch her go, feeling like with each step, the gulf between us grows.*
*I give Abigail the tour of the place. Show her our room, the kitchen, the living room. I explain each modern appliance and convenience, and she listens intently. I pause outside Tara's closed bedroom door, wondering if I should knock. I don't.*
*We return to the living room and settle down on the couch. Abigail's gaze sweeps over everything, and I know she's bursting with questions to ask. But she keeps them all inside for now. For now, it's just the two of us, finding solace in each other.*
*"She doesn't blame you, you know."*
*I turn my head to face Abigail. "Wait, can you read minds too? You didn't tell me about that one."*
*"I'm not the only one who's easy to read, Madison. We both know you did everything you could. Even if it takes time for her to process it, I’m sure she’s as aware of that as I am deep down. I might not know her that well, but I know that she's a good person. You are too. Morgan gave her life so the three of us could survive. We should honor that gift. All of us."*
*A thud behind us makes me jump as I whirl towards the stairs, half-expecting Evelyn to jump out at us again. It’s going to be a long time before I can shake that fear.*
*But it’s not her, of course. It’s Tara, back again. She reaches out for her one of her suitcases, making faltering eye contact with us. I can tell she’s still not sure how to feel. But the fact that she came back down here is a good sign, right? I want to reach out and offer some real help, but… I know she has to do this at her own pace. Maybe, if I just reach out a little…*
*"Hey, um… Abigail and I were going to maybe order some pizza for dinner. Do you want us to order some for you too? We can get hawaiian."*
*She looks at me like I’m speaking another language. The awkward silence makes me immediately regret my offer. But after a moment… she smiles. It’s small, only on her face for a moment. But it’s real. The first real one I’ve seen from her since we left Eysenfeld.*
*"You hate it when I order hawaiian. The last time I did, you talked about how it was ’unnatural’ for ten minutes straight."*
*"Is that a no?"*
*That fleeting smile widens, and this time it stays there. By my side Abigail smiles too. Tara sniffles, and for a moment it looks like she’s going to cry. But with a small cough, she suppresses it. Instead, she gives me a small nod.*
*"There’s no way I’m missing out on a chance to make you experience the joy of pineapple on pizza. You’re on."*
*Having said her piece, Tara turns and heads back upstairs with her bag. I can tell the distance between us is still going to take a long time to fully repair. But I know that as long we're on the same team, we'll get through it. We've proven that, time and time again. We all have our share of fears for the future and regrets toward the past. But we all have time to face them, and to grow. Because that's the best way to honor Morgan’s sacrifice. We’re going to live our lives to the fullest, without regrets.*
*Madison, look! There it is!*
*Abigail reaches out the open window to point at the ocean, sparkling in the summer sun. My heart pounds as I hear the pure joy in her voice. The instant the train pulls to a complete stop, she and Tara bolt for the door. Morgan grins at me and stands to follow them. I'm right behind her.*
*All right, gang! This is it!*
*All four gals head to the beach for a day of fun in the sun. Tara's leaping for joy while wearing a "Taranormal" t-shirt, complete with UFO and rainbow. Maddie and Abigail are both in light beachwear. Morgan has cut her hair short, and watches them with a smile.*
Tara: "Smell that air! Feel that ocean breeze! Look at those crashing waves! I told you this place was awesome!"
*She looks back at us to make sure we're all as overjoyed as she is. With all her excitement, you'd think she was the one who had never seen a beach before.*
Abigail: "This is… "
*Her voice trails off as her head swivels around, still trying to take it all in. I silently step up behind her and take her hand. She doesn’t look at me, but the small squeeze she gives my hand is all I need to know she appreciates it.*
Madison: "Pretty incredible, huh?"
*She closes her eyes and tilts her head up towards the sun, taking in the summer glow. *
Abigail: "It's better than I ever dared to imagine."
*For a moment, I'm reminded of the glow that surrounded her the first time we read together. Was that the first time my heart stopped when I looked at her? It’s happened so often that I’ve lost count.*
*By now, I've known her as a human for longer than I knew her as a ghost. But she's still got that same otherworldly beauty that she had back then.*
Abigail: "Thank you, Madison."
*Abigail looks back to me and smiles.*
Abigail: "For everything."
*I know the kiss is coming before Abigail even starts to move. And I'm more than ready for it.*
*I wrap my arms around her and let her lips press against mine, our bodies perfectly pressed together.*
Abigail: "You were worth the wait."
*We touch our foreheads together, fingers intertwined. There's no need to say anything else.*
*For once, Tara waits for us to finish before interjecting. But the instant we separate, she's back to her boisterous self.*
Tara: "Are you guys coming, or what?"
*Morgan puts her arm around Tara's shoulders as she waves us on. Not missing a beat, Tara pushes her long hair to the side so Morgan can kiss her cheek.*
Tara: "Come on, I'll race ya there."
Abigail: "You're on."
*Those two turned out to have a surprising amount in common. Whatever store of energy they both draw from, it seems to be infinite.*
*Morgan and I go at a much more similar pace, although even she’s more enthusiastic about going along with Tara’s craziness. Love does that, I guess.*
Morgan: "You go ahead, babe. I'll catch up."
Madison: "Me too. My swimsuit definitely isn't built for sprinting."
*Abigail gives me a nod and another quick peck. Tara tries to do the same, but Morgan pulls her in for a full-on kiss. When she pulls away, Tara looks completely stunned.*
Morgan: "Better hurry, Abigail. Don't waste the head start."
*That’s the first time I’ve ever seen a kiss used to give someone a tactical advantage. I can respect that.*
*Abigail takes off running while Tara blinks away her daze before dashing after her.*
Tara: "No fair!"
Abigail: "Yes it is! You have longer legs than me!"
*Morgan and I are left in the dust, both chuckling at our overzealous partners. I doubt either of us expected someone so energetic to be such a perfect fit.*
Madison: "We should probably go after them."
Morgan: "Lead the way."
*We both take off behind our girlfriends, entering the water and rushing to their arms.*
*I can barely feel the cold of the water as Abigail's warmth envelops me.*
Abigail: "And here I thought you were going to keep me waiting again."
*We lean in for a kiss at the same time, our lips pressing together in a deep, warm union.*
Madison: "Don't you worry, Abigail."
Madison: "I'll always be right behind you."
*The smile she gives me puts the sunlight to shame.*
Abigail: "I know, Madison."
Abigail: "I know."
Tara: "So next up, we’ve got bigfootforever, who asks: "are you single?""
*I roll my eyes. We always get a few comments like this when we do a livestream. They never seem to bother Tara, though.*
Tara: "Yes, but only because I’m saving myself for Nessie. Next…."
*As she looks at the next question, her eyes widen and her mouth turns upward into a worryingly smug grin.*
Tara: "Our next question comes from damngoodcoffee who asks: is MADDIE single?"
*Oh, no.*
*As she turns off frame to look at me, I instantly raise my hands in protest.*
Madison: "Tara, don’t-"
*Before I can finish my sentence, she leaps from her chair and grabs my arm, pulling me towards her and nearly knocking the camera over in the process.*
*As the shock starts to wear off, I struggle to get away. It’s no use; her vise-like grip is impossible to escape from.*
Madison: "H-hey!"
Tara: "You’d think someone this cute would have a girlfriend by now."
Madison: "Tara, I swear to God…"
*I continue to fight in vain to escape, but she’s locked in.*
Tara: "But she’s available, ladies! You’ve still got a chance!"
Tara: "I’ll pass any love-filled confessions on to her, I promise."
Madison: "Please do not do that."
*Her bit finished, she lets me go and I retreat to my chair. She knows I hate being on camera.*
*But I suppose it can’t be helped. When she’s in the zone, nothing can stop her. It’s impressive in its own right.*
Tara: "Let’s see….clownprincess20 asks, "what is your favorite west coast cryptid?""
Tara: "THE FRESNO NIGHTCRAWLER!"
Tara: "IT’S JUST PANTS! IT’S JUST WALKING PANTS! I LOVE IT!"
Tara: "Hang on, I’m posting a link to my video on it. Take this opportunity to educate yourself on what’s really important in life."
*Looking at the clock, I motion for her to wrap it up. We still haven’t gotten to the trip details.*
Tara: "Okay, looks like we’ve got time for one more… funssassin says: Tara, what’s the gayest cryptid?"
*Her mouth widening into her trademark grin, she jabs her thumbs toward herself before poking two fingers out toward the camera.*
Tara: "Me, obviously."
*Watching the chat on my phone, I can’t help smiling as I see the feed flooded with hearts and tiny rainbow flags. She always knows how to finish strong.*
*Once the chat starts to calm down, she puts on a mock-serious face, pursing her lips and furrowing her brow slightly. If I’m being honest, that’s probably the closest to actually being serious she could ever possibly get.*
Tara: "Now then. The reason I’ve called you all here today is to give you a few details about mine and Maddie’s upcoming trip to…"
*She looks at me for help. I don’t even know why we bother rehearsing details.*
Madison: "Eysenfeld."
Tara: "EYSENFELD!"
*I give her an absentminded thumbs up, not looking up from my phone. Someone has to handle the actual logistics of this thing.*
Tara: "Obviously, this isn’t our first supernatural expedition. I’m sure you all remember our incredibly thrilling Mothman hunt."
Madison: "You mean running around West Virginia in the dark and nearly getting arrested for trespassing without finding a shred of evidence?"
Tara: "I wouldn’t say we didn’t find any evidence! I just think it’s impossible to verify whether or not what we found was conclusive proof or not. Anyway…"
Tara: "The reason this trip is a little different is because we’re going to be gone for a while. Our host, Morgan--"
Tara: "Shout out to you if you’re watching this, Morgan!"
Tara: "--was kind enough to offer us an extended stay in the area to make sure we take in every last bit of magic the forest has to offer!"
*The reminder of the time commitment fills me with a wave of regret. I really should have put my foot down harder when I said I didn’t want to go.*
*Taking a week-long trip here and there is one thing, but to uproot and move to some cabin in the middle of the freezing wilderness is a big ask. Even for her.*
*But I suppose, given the circumstances, this should at least soften the blow.*
Tara: "The other big difference for this trip is, and I hope you’re all bracing yourselves: we won’t be uploading videos until we get back."
*I watch her try not to smile as crying emojis fill the chat. Nothing more validating than knowing people are going to miss you while you’re gone, I guess.*
Tara: "But I promise we’re gonna bring back something that’ll be worth the wait!"
*As she launches into an in-depth history of supernatural forest phenomena, I start to tune out. I’ve got some much more immediate problems to think about; namely how the hell I’m going to keep Tara in check during this trip.*
*The last thing I need is for her to cause trouble while we’re stuck in the middle of the woods. The train coming once a month means if things go south that could lead to weeks of headaches and awkwardness. Or worse.*
*It would be so much easier to just let her go off on her own, let her clean up her own mess. I’d give just about anything to stay back here, even if it would be significantly more boring without her around.*
*But cleaning up her mess is basically my job description. For now, anyway.*
Tara: "No, Sasquatch sightings are typically reported in more mountainous areas. But I’ll keep my eye out for one!"
Tara: "Oh, one last thing, and this is very important:"
Tara: "While I’m gone, just remember that I seriously do love each and every one of you. None of this would be possible without all of you, and not being able to have you all be a part of my life for a whole month is going to be as hard for me as it is for you!"
*Another flood of hearts and pride flags.*
*I can’t help being a little jealous of the fact that she can say things like that and actually mean it. I can’t imagine being that invested in something like this.*
*Even if she’s probably overestimating how important she might be in these people’s eyes, I’m sure it means something to them.*
*Just another reason for me to feel guilty about what I’m about to do. I suppose I'm thankful that I don't mean as much to them as she does, or this would be even harder.*
Tara: "So, until I return… Stay spooky, everyone!"
*She waits for one last burst of of reverent comments before ending the stream. The responses are probably exactly what she was hoping for.*
*We love you Tara!!!!!!!!!*
*nPls don’t go TTATT*
*nnSo cute <333333*
*No wonder her ego is as massive as it is. Much as I hate to admit it, I’m sure I’d be the same way.*
*She flicks the camera off and leaps from her chair, clearly still filled with energy.*
Tara: "OKAY! Let’s get ready for this thing!"
Madison: "What do you mean?"
Tara: "Well, for starters, I need to start packing."
Madison: "You haven’t started packing yet? We leave on Monday!"
Tara: "Oh yeah, you’re right. I can pack then."
Madison: "PACK. NOW."
Tara: "Fiiiiiine."
*Her burst of energy now effectively redirected, she starts dashing around the room, randomly grabbing clothes off the floor and throwing them into a suitcase. I need to stop letting her leave her laundry in my room.*
Tara: "Do you know where my meds are?"
Madison: "That’s one of those things you really shouldn’t have to ask me, you know. That’s kind of important."
Tara: "Do you though?"
*I let out a long sigh and rub my temples. I guarantee that Tara-induced headaches are going to be the death of me.*
Madison: "They’re downstairs in the kitchen, on the counter."
Tara: "You’re the best manager ever."
*The words hit me like a slap across the face, my stomach knotting with guilt. I still haven’t told her that I’m quitting after this.*
*I keep meaning to, really, but… I just know she’s not going to understand. She’s going to take it personally.*
*There’s no way I can tell her I’m quitting without her thinking there was something she could have done to stop it.*
*I can’t just wait until we’re already out there to tell her this is going to be the last time I do this with her. I have to do it beforehand.*
*I should just do it now, get it over with. Tell her it’s about school, not her. Tell her I’m conflicted about the whole thing and it’s not something I’m taking lightly. Tell her I just need to move forward with my life.*
*Not that any of it would make a difference.*
Tara: "Hey, Mads?"
Madison: "Huh?"
Tara: "Are you okay? You just kinda spaced out for a minute there."
Madison: "Yeah, I’m fine. Sorry. Just thinking about the trip."
Tara: "You and me both! It’s gonna be so awesome!"
Madison: "Yeah. Yeah, I’m sure it will."
*With that, she’s off again, nearly vibrating with excitement as she tears the room apart grabbing all sorts of random items that are apparently extremely necessary for the trip. I can only imagine what she's going to do in her own room.*
*I can’t tell her now. I don’t want to ruin this moment for her. Tonight, maybe. Or tomorrow.*
*I just hope that at least a part of her understands.*
